=== idx: 932 ===
Gold supporting: transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ) is generally observed as more rapid or more efficient removal of certain types of dna damage from the transcribed strands of expressed genes compared with the nontranscribed strands.
LLM supporting:  the context provided does not explicitly mention the specific gene strand targeted by transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ). however, based on the understanding of tcr as a subpathway of nucleotide excision repair ( ner ) that is activated when the transcription machinery encounters a lesion in the dna template, it is well - established that tcr primarily targets the non - template ( or coding ) strand of dna. this is because the rna polymerase, which is transcribing the dna, can stall at the site of the lesion, leading to the recruitment of the ner machinery. the ner complex then recognizes and removes the damaged segment of the non - template strand, allowing the transcription to resume. this mechanism ensures that the integrity of the transcribed mrna is maintained, as the repair process preferentially addresses the strand that is being actively copied into mrna.
